Historical Blender stage/call evidence audit, English at most 1200 words. These are complete selected event/call files, not image or full geometry attachments. Cite source IDs, exact original paths and one-based lines. Distinguish actual recorded build/patch/render/validation transitions, failed commands and merely proposed actions; teacher advice, student execution, acceptance and quality are separate. Identify material evidence and counterexamples regarding stage ordering, object/part relations, pose/camera/lighting/material checks, rerenders, failures, H/M or mechanism updates and acceptance decisions. Do not equate an ok flag, render completion or AABB bounds with visual fidelity/physical contact. Inputs include original/publication copies; do not count them as independent trials or generalize all scenes. This audit never feeds code students or capability updates. No new solution or code.
